This framework also organises the continuation of minimum wage allowance for reducing the burden to employers due to a minimum wage increase in 2019. According to this, minimum wage allowance for 150 lira per month, 500 per month, and more than 500 per month for businesses with an average number of insurance holders reported to the SGK will be covered by the treasury. We care and support the approach of the Nationalist Movement Party to reduce the cost of not being negatively affected by our economy's competitiveness, causing unregistered employment and non-employment of workers. The total cost of minimum wage to the employer is 26.1% higher than $621.50. The minimum wage is $2.020, but the employer costs $3,006. Tax and bonus cuts on minimum wage reduce employees' net wage, while also increase the cost of employers and prepare grounds for off-the-book employment. The quality of the minimum wage and non-registered employment relationship is not because the minimum wage is high, but because of the excess of cuts. Taxation of the minimum wage set only by considering the needs of employees is incompatible with the understanding of the social state. Therefore, lifting the tax burden of minimum wage is an economic and social obligation. In addition to the burden on employers, there have been significant increases in many areas, including BUG-KUR bonuses and general health insurance premiums and administrative fines. The lowest tradesmen's BUG-KUR bonus increased by 26.1 per cent to $883 per month. If the shop doesn't have a bonus, it's $755 a month with a five-point bonus discount. It will be very difficult for our tradesman, especially our small tradesman, to pay for it. Many tradesmen can't take advantage of a bonus discount because they can't pay their premiums, and if they owe more than two months, they can't get health care. It's the same with our farmer. The lowest agricultural rate increased by 31.1 per cent to $765 per month. If the farmer doesn't have a bonus, it's $654 a month with a five-point bonus discount. It's very difficult for our farmer to handle this burden. Because of economic difficulties, our tradesman and farmer face serious problems. I've said it a couple of times, and once again, let me make it very clear that the presidential system is the traditional American system, which everyone counts first among the countries in which the system is relatively democratic. The most fundamental feature of the American system is that party discipline is extremely lax, that transitionalness is strong, so when the political opposition party says something right, you can develop politics in a transitional position, not in a party decision. But in countries where there are almost parties like Turkey, where there are camps, when there are two people together, when they're together, when even when they're talking together, they're almost at the root of the fanatical party, and where politics is being investigated, where tension is at its premium, the presidential system only serves to divide. Now, there's a system. If we're going to try and learn this system, it shouldn't be politics' choice at least to try and learn from it at a very high price. Of course, there's a contradiction between theory and practice everywhere, and sometimes you test the truth of the theory with practice.
